HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0f0e2608023c8857bfe7","text":"1. **Measured capacity becoming a path.** Lexical evidence: ق د ر B001/B003/B005 (measure, capacity, deliberative arranging), ه د ي B001/B002 (guidance and course). Structural cue: the fa- sequence after 87:2 makes تقدير the condition for هداية. Assigned role: measure sets limits and capacities; guidance directs within those limits. Abductive move: direction is meaningful only when the recipient has a bounded capacity and route. Reading change: \"destined\" is not fatalistic closure but calibrated enablement toward a way.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1700b44deeea397015e2","text":"2. **Constriction as part of direction.** Lexical evidence: قدر B004 narrowing to a scant measure; يسر B001 ease in 87:8; دنو B002 nearer/lower life in 87:16. Structural cue: measure sits before later easing and before the critique of preferring the nearer life. Assigned role: limits define the path and prevent false expansion. Abductive move: the packet gives constriction and ease; I infer that divine making-easy does not erase boundaries but makes the right bounded course passable. Reading change: \"measured, then guided\" includes restriction as mercy, not only abundance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2a1b08007e5ba4e81bbe","text":"2. **Determination includes productive limits.** `ق د ر/B004` gives constriction to a scant measure, alongside `ق د ر/B001` for a bounded extent. Pasture’s reduction to chaff in 87:4–5 and the exception to non-forgetting in 87:6–7 activate limitation as part of the surrounding order. The limiting branch assigns edges and termini to what is guided. I infer that depletion and exception exemplify boundedness rather than failures of the plan. The reading changes from unlimited allotment to guidance operating through deliberately finite capacities and phases.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B003","root_001583/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_333027a3638b7d83e823","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:3:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B003\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ön sıra ve adak çağrışımları, yol göstermeyi ibadete doğru önden götüren bir sevk olarak düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5633f5287328140ca428","text":"3. **Guidance as leading front and offering.** Lexical evidence: هدي B003 leading front part; هدي B005 offering sent to sanctuary; سبح B008 sacred precinct place-name; صلو B003 worship in 87:15. Structural cue: the surah moves from command to path to ritual prayer and older scriptures. Assigned role: guidance can be a leading sign and a devotional sending. Abductive move: the packet supplies \"leading\" and \"offering\"; I infer a ritualized path because later remembrance/prayer embodies the guidance. Reading change: the ayah can sound like being led ahead toward worship, not simply receiving information.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5a872e9c7b6795593b0e","text":"3. **Guidance can be a proportioned gift.** `ه د ي/B004` is a gracious gift sent to someone close. Within the window, 87:6 gives causative recitation and 87:8 gives facilitation toward ease; these form a recipient-oriented sequence after `قَدَّرَ`. The gift branch makes guidance something tailored and delivered, with measure determining its fit. I infer that recitation and ease are instances or means of the gift; the packet does not name them as its contents. The ayah can therefore read as measure followed by a fitted benefaction, not only abstract direction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B001","root_001205/B005","root_001205/B006","root_001583/B001","root_001583/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_70078c12580be363549d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:3:2\",\"root_001205\",[\"B001\",\"B005\",\"B006\"]],[\"87:3:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Belirlenen ölçü, her varlığı kapasitesine uygun bir yola sevk eden ayarlı yönlendirmeye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B004","root_001583/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8d462d3f232347068619","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:3:2\",\"root_001205\",[\"B004\"]],[\"87:3:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ölçünün daraltıcı sınırları da yol göstermenin parçasıdır; kolaylaştırma, bu sınırları kaldırmak yerine doğru yolu geçilebilir kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B006","root_001583/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_91a91e0128806e402c41","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:3:2\",\"root_001205\",[\"B006\"]],[\"87:3:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yol gösterme, 87:6 ve 87:8'de okuma ve kolaylaştırma olarak alıcıya uygun biçimde ulaştırılan bir armağan niteliği kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_96c25527c5ba3ecb4798","text":"87:8 retrospectively changed \"فهدى\": if the addressee is made easy for ease, guidance is not only direction but facilitation. 87:18-19 made the course older than the immediate speaker, linking قدر/هدى to the \"first sheets.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B005","root_001583/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9a8e521cd7df8426f2bb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:3:2\",\"root_001205\",[\"B005\"]],[\"87:3:3\",\"root_001583\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İlk sayfalara yapılan son gönderme, burada belirlenen yolu kuşaklar boyunca korunan bir düzen olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ae8ef8945001d865dc39","text":"87:6 and 87:8 made `ه د ي/B004` newly legible as a fitted gift: recitation is delivered and the recipient is facilitated toward ease. Conversely, 87:4–5 and the exception in 87:7 showed that `ق د ر` includes limits and endpoints, correcting any reading of measure as simple abundance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_baabcb9c3301ffa5e453","text":"4. **Measured guidance as archival continuity.** Lexical evidence: قدر B005 calculation; هدي B002 course/manner; صحف B002 written sheet and ءول B001 firstness in 87:18-19. Structural cue: the final ayat locate \"this\" in earlier sheets. Assigned role: guidance is a repeatable course preserved across records. Abductive move: the packet gives course plus written transmission; I infer that the measured way is stable enough to be inscribed and inherited. Reading change: the ayah's guidance becomes historically continuous.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c350a9a09e03e05e5326","text":"2. **Narrowing that produces ease.** Lexical evidence: ق د ر B004 (narrowing to scant measure), ي س ر B001/B002 in 87:8 (ease and small amount), د ن و B002 in 87:16 (nearer/first side). Structural cue: قدر precedes \"We will ease you toward ease.\" Assigned role: constriction or exact measure becomes the channel that makes the later path easy. Abductive move: a narrowed channel can reduce wandering and therefore enable movement. Reading change: guidance may include limitation, not merely expansion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d4c293eaa262e911d8b6","text":"3. **Leading-front imagery.** Lexical evidence: ه د ي B003 (leading front part), خ ر ج B001/B002 in 87:4 (bringing out), ر ع ي B001/B002 (pasture/shepherding). Abductive move: the packet's emergence and reminder scenes share a guide-ahead mechanism. Reading change: guidance reads as being led out into a visible course.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001205/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e13e76f8e3a327f100a5","text":"1. **Measure becomes a routed course.** `ق د ر/B005` is arrangement by estimate and deliberation, while `ه د ي/B002` is the course, manner, and intended direction of a matter. Measure sets capacities and relations; guidance routes them through an ordered development. I infer that proportion enables direction, which is the unstated causal force of `فـ`. Abductive move: the packet supplies measure and guidance; I infer that guidance is calibrated rather than generic.
